# rl_hardware_300.dice, translated by tddlog's scripts/dice2alea.py.
module AleaProgram
using Alea
# Julia would spend more time optimizing the long straight-line functions
# below than running them takes: they are interpreted, and Alea's own
# methods compile as usual.
Base.Experimental.@compiler_options compile=min optimize=0 infer=false

const EV = Ref{AnyBool}(true)
observe!(path, c) = (EV[] = EV[] & (!path | c); true)

function shr(x::DistUInt{W}, n::Integer) where W
    k = min(n, W)
    DistUInt{W}(vcat(fill(false, k), x.bits[1:W - k]))
end

components(x::Tuple) = reduce(vcat, map(components, x); init = Any[])
components(x) = Any[x]
width(::DistUInt{W}) where W = W

function warmup()
    a = flip(0.7)
    x = discrete(DistUInt{2}, [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4])
    y = uniform(DistUInt{3}, 1, 6)
    z = ifelse(a, x + DistUInt{2}(1), x - DistUInt{2}(1))
    t = ifelse(flip(0.4), (a, z), (!a, x * x))
    e = prob_equals(z, DistUInt{2}(2)) | (x < z) | xor(a, flip(0.2))
    pr(flip(0.7))
    pr(a, z, y, t...; evidence = e)
    nothing
end

    x4168_g298 = x4167_z298c === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4167_z298c === false ? x4160_d298 : ifelse(x4167_z298c, DistUInt{2}(0), x4160_d298)
    x4169_t298 = (x4166_u298 + x4168_g298)
    x4170_z298d = flip(0.0001)
    x4171_f298 = x4170_z298d === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4170_z298d === false ? x4168_g298 : ifelse(x4170_z298d, DistUInt{2}(0), x4168_g298)
    x4172_c298 = (x4171_f298 + DistUInt{2}(1))
    x4173_z299a = flip(0.0001)
    x4174_d299 = x4173_z299a === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4173_z299a === false ? x4172_c298 : ifelse(x4173_z299a, DistUInt{2}(0), x4172_c298)
    x4175_x299 = (x4164_on298 & prob_equals(x4174_d299, DistUInt{2}(0)))
    x4176_r299 = x4175_x299 === true ? x4169_t298 : x4175_x299 === false ? x4162_r298 : ifelse(x4175_x299, x4169_t298, x4162_r298)
    x4177_done299 = (x4163_done298 | x4175_x299)
    x4178_on299 = (x4164_on298 & !(x4175_x299))
    x4179_z299b = flip(0.0001)
    x4180_u299 = x4179_z299b === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4179_z299b === false ? x4169_t298 : ifelse(x4179_z299b, DistUInt{2}(0), x4169_t298)
    x4181_z299c = flip(0.0001)
    x4182_g299 = x4181_z299c === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4181_z299c === false ? x4174_d299 : ifelse(x4181_z299c, DistUInt{2}(0), x4174_d299)
    x4183_t299 = (x4180_u299 + x4182_g299)
    x4184_z299d = flip(0.0001)
    x4185_f299 = x4184_z299d === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4184_z299d === false ? x4182_g299 : ifelse(x4184_z299d, DistUInt{2}(0), x4182_g299)
    x4186_c299 = (x4185_f299 + DistUInt{2}(1))
    x4187_z300a = flip(0.0001)
    x4188_d300 = x4187_z300a === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4187_z300a === false ? x4186_c299 : ifelse(x4187_z300a, DistUInt{2}(0), x4186_c299)
    x4189_x300 = (x4178_on299 & prob_equals(x4188_d300, DistUInt{2}(0)))
    x4190_r300 = x4189_x300 === true ? x4183_t299 : x4189_x300 === false ? x4176_r299 : ifelse(x4189_x300, x4183_t299, x4176_r299)
    x4191_done300 = (x4177_done299 | x4189_x300)
    x4192_on300 = (x4178_on299 & !(x4189_x300))
    x4193_z300b = flip(0.0001)
    x4194_u300 = x4193_z300b === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4193_z300b === false ? x4183_t299 : ifelse(x4193_z300b, DistUInt{2}(0), x4183_t299)
    x4195_z300c = flip(0.0001)
    x4196_g300 = x4195_z300c === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4195_z300c === false ? x4188_d300 : ifelse(x4195_z300c, DistUInt{2}(0), x4188_d300)
    x4197_t300 = (x4194_u300 + x4196_g300)
    x4198_z300d = flip(0.0001)
    x4199_f300 = x4198_z300d === true ? DistUInt{2}(0) : x4198_z300d === false ? x4196_g300 : ifelse(x4198_z300d, DistUInt{2}(0), x4196_g300)
    x4200_c300 = (x4199_f300 + DistUInt{2}(1))
    if x4191_done300 !== false
     x4201_result = prob_equals(x4190_r300, DistUInt{2}(0))
     if x4201_result !== true
      x4202_result = prob_equals(x4190_r300, DistUInt{2}(1))
      if x4202_result !== true
       x4203_result = prob_equals(x4190_r300, DistUInt{2}(2))
       if x4203_result !== true
        x4204_result = prob_equals(x4190_r300, DistUInt{2}(3))
        x4205_result = x4204_result === true ? DistUInt{3}(3) : x4204_result === false ? DistUInt{3}(4) : ifelse(x4204_result, DistUInt{3}(3), DistUInt{3}(4))
       end
       x4206_result = x4203_result === true ? DistUInt{3}(2) : x4203_result === false ? x4205_result : ifelse(x4203_result, DistUInt{3}(2), x4205_result)
      end
      x4207_result = x4202_result === true ? DistUInt{3}(1) : x4202_result === false ? x4206_result : ifelse(x4202_result, DistUInt{3}(1), x4206_result)
     end
     x4208_result = x4201_result === true ? DistUInt{3}(0) : x4201_result === false ? x4207_result : ifelse(x4201_result, DistUInt{3}(0), x4207_result)
    end
    x4209_result = x4191_done300 === true ? x4208_result : x4191_done300 === false ? DistUInt{3}(4) : ifelse(x4191_done300, x4208_result, DistUInt{3}(4))
    return x4209_result
end

function dice_main()
    (x394_d29, x396_r29, x397_done29, x398_on29, x400_u29,) = chunk_1()
    (x788_r57, x789_done57, x790_on57, x795_t57, x800_d58,) = chunk_2(x394_d29, x396_r29, x397_done29, x398_on29, x400_u29)
    (x1194_r86, x1195_done86, x1196_on86, x1198_u86, x1200_g86,) = chunk_3(x788_r57, x789_done57, x790_on57, x795_t57, x800_d58)
    (x1587_done114, x1588_on114, x1593_t114, x1598_d115, x1599_x115, x1600_r115,) = chunk_4(x1194_r86, x1195_done86, x1196_on86, x1198_u86, x1200_g86)
    (x1992_r143, x1993_done143, x1994_on143, x1998_g143, x1999_t143, x2000_z143d,) = chunk_5(x1587_done114, x1588_on114, x1593_t114, x1598_d115, x1599_x115, x1600_r115)
    (x2391_t171, x2396_d172, x2398_r172, x2399_done172, x2400_on172,) = chunk_6(x1992_r143, x1993_done143, x1994_on143, x1998_g143, x1999_t143, x2000_z143d)
    (x2790_r200, x2791_done200, x2792_on200, x2797_t200, x2800_c200,) = chunk_7(x2391_t171, x2396_d172, x2398_r172, x2399_done172, x2400_on172)
    (x3194_d229, x3196_r229, x3197_done229, x3198_on229, x3200_u229,) = chunk_8(x2790_r200, x2791_done200, x2792_on200, x2797_t200, x2800_c200)
    (x3588_r257, x3589_done257, x3590_on257, x3595_t257, x3600_d258,) = chunk_9(x3194_d229, x3196_r229, x3197_done229, x3198_on229, x3200_u229)
    (x3994_r286, x3995_done286, x3996_on286, x3998_u286, x4000_g286,) = chunk_10(x3588_r257, x3589_done257, x3590_on257, x3595_t257, x3600_d258)
    x4209_result = chunk_11(x3994_r286, x3995_done286, x3996_on286, x3998_u286, x4000_g286)
    return x4209_result
end

function report(k, x, d)
    if x isa AnyBool
        println("alea ", k, " true ", d[true])
        println("alea ", k, " false ", d[false])
    else
        for v in 0:(2^width(x) - 1)
            println("alea ", k, " ", v, " ", d[v])
        end
    end
end

function run_program()
    EV[] = true
    stats = @timed begin
        comps = components(dice_main())
        dists = pr(comps...; evidence = EV[])
        (comps, length(comps) == 1 ? [dists] : dists)
    end
    comps, dists = stats.value
    for k in eachindex(comps)
        report(k, comps[k], dists[k])
    end
    println("alea_seconds ", stats.time)
    println("alea_compile_seconds ", get(stats, :compile_time, NaN))
end

warmup()
task = Task(run_program, 1 << 30)
schedule(task)
wait(task)
end
